		"pay_page_协议正文_131" "5.6 [Account Security Obligations] Users must not participate in unofficial private transactions, nor disclose or deliver security information such as account passwords, verification codes, tokens, login QR codes, payment vouchers, or device control to others, and must not allow others to remotely log in, operate on their behalf, or bind payment tools. 		"pay_page_协议正文_132" "5.7 [Assumption of Risk] Unofficial private transactions are not services provided or endorsed by Yunhai Youchuang. Yunhai Youchuang does not participate in the listing, matchmaking, pricing, guaranteeing, escrow, delivery, settlement, or dispute resolution thereof. Any disputes and losses arising therefrom, including non-delivery, discrepancies, fraud, account theft, financial loss, data leakage, revocation of benefits, account restrictions or bans, and data rollbacks, shall be resolved and borne solely by the participating parties in accordance with the law."
		"pay_page_协议正文_133" "5.8 [Dispute Reporting and Scope of Assistance] Users should immediately cease payments and account operations, preserve chat, order, payment, and login records as evidence, and promptly contact payment institutions, third-party platforms, or public security authorities. Within the bounds permitted by law and technical feasibility, Yunhai Youchuang may impose temporary restrictions, conduct investigations, handle violations, or cooperate with competent authorities; however, this does not constitute endorsement or guarantee of private transactions, nor does Yunhai Youchuang promise to recover funds, restore accounts or data, reissue benefits, or provide advance compensation."
		"pay_page_协议正文_134" "6.5 For goods containing class cosmetics, cosmetic stats, random draw results, or other season-exclusive benefits, Yunhai Youchuang will make reasonable efforts to indicate, prior to payment, the associated season, the retention method after the season ends, whether they carry over to subsequent seasons, and whether the benefits are account-level permanent benefits."
		"pay_page_协议正文_135" "6.6 Within the scope permitted by normal business processes and technical conditions, Yunhai Youchuang retains business records related to orders, distributions, and event rules, which may serve as the basis for Yunhai Youchuang to handle orders, complaints, and disputes. Yunhai Youchuang may provide access to view rules during the payment process as operational needs require."
		"pay_page_协议正文_136" "7.7 For class cosmetics, cosmetic stats, or other season-exclusive virtual items explicitly marked as exclusive to a specific season, the obtained records in the corresponding season save will in principle be retained, and may continue to be used according to the rules of that season as long as the season map or related services are still provided and no other invalidation events are triggered. The end of the season itself does not equate to the deletion of the corresponding season save."
		"pay_page_协议正文_137" "7.8 Unless explicitly stated on the page, event rules, or announcements as eligible for carryover, season-exclusive virtual items, cosmetic stats, draw results, and usage qualifications from a specific season will not automatically carry over, transfer, duplicate, or convert into items, stats, or qualifications usable in subsequent seasons. Goods, prize pools, stats, validity periods, and terms of use for subsequent seasons are subject to the pages and rules of those subsequent seasons."
		"pay_page_协议正文_138" "7.9 The stats of season-exclusive cosmetics take effect only within the specified season and applicable map scope. Obtained stat records for the corresponding season remain saved according to that season's rules, but do not automatically take effect in subsequent seasons merely because the user possesses such records."
		"pay_page_协议正文_139" "7.10 Content explicitly marked on the page as account-level permanent benefits or cross-season benefits does not constitute season-exclusive virtual items and will be handled according to the corresponding product page and event rules. The Eternal Voucher Pool is a voucher recovery cap and is not equivalent to cash, immediate refunds, or unconditional reimbursement of consumed vouchers."
		"pay_page_协议正文_140" "7.11 Upon season transition, Yunhai Youchuang may establish new season saves and rules for subsequent seasons. Unless explicitly stated on the page or announcement, season-exclusive assets are not considered automatically carried over to subsequent seasons; related goods, features, and rules for subsequent seasons are subject to the published content of the respective season."
		"pay_page_协议正文_141" "8.6 System records retained by Yunhai Youchuang in the normal course of business—such as the time of user participation in randomized acquisition events, consumption, prize pool versions, randomized results, and distribution records—may serve as the basis for handling related disputes. Users with questions regarding their orders or draw results may contact customer service channels, and Yunhai Youchuang will review and handle the matter at its discretion based on retained records."
		"pay_page_协议正文_142" "9.4 Seasonal benefits are handled upon season transition according to the season attribution and carryover rules displayed prior to payment. Benefits not explicitly marked as cross-season or account-level permanent benefits are not deemed to automatically extend into subsequent seasons; the scope of retention for records obtained in a specific season shall be governed by Article 7."
		"pay_page_协议正文_143" "10.5 If a user raises a dispute regarding the duration of season-exclusive benefits, the retention method of the corresponding season save, or the boundaries of subsequent season carryover, Yunhai Youchuang may handle the matter at its discretion based on retained records, unconsumed vouchers, used benefits, actual service conditions, and treatment standards for similar users. Settlement methods may include refunds, equivalent replacement benefits, retaining records of the corresponding season, or other solutions deemed reasonable by Yunhai Youchuang."
		"pay_page_协议正文_144" "14.5 A season transition does not inherently constitute the termination of services or season saves for the corresponding season. Unless otherwise explicitly stated on the page or announcement, season-exclusive items and stat records will remain preserved in the corresponding season save; the rule that they do not automatically carry over to subsequent seasons shall follow Article 7."
		"pay_page_协议正文_145" "17.4 [Limitation of Liability for Unofficial Private Transactions] To the extent permitted by law, Yunhai Youchuang bears no counterparty or contractual fulfillment liability for unofficial private transactions referred to in Article 5 or losses resulting from the user's failure to fulfill account security obligations, nor does it have any obligation to refund, reissue, compensate, guarantee, provide advance compensation, or restore data; except for losses directly caused by intentional misconduct or gross negligence of Yunhai Youchuang, and liabilities that cannot be excluded or limited under applicable laws and regulations."
		"pay_page_协议正文_146" "18.3 For major changes involving payment prices, product contents, season attribution, validity periods, draw probabilities, retention methods of corresponding season saves, boundaries of subsequent season carryover, refunds, or primary user rights and obligations, Yunhai Youchuang will announce them through reasonable means, specifying the effective date and scope of application. Completed transactions shall be handled in accordance with the rules published at the time of payment and relevant laws and regulations."
		"pay_page_协议正文_147" "20.7 Business records retained by Yunhai Youchuang in the normal course of business—such as order records, distribution records, draw records, page and rule versions—may serve as the basis for handling related disputes. If certain records are missing or incomplete due to technical reasons or exceeding reasonable retention periods, Yunhai Youchuang will handle the matter at its discretion based on available records, without inherently assuming liability for refunds, compensation, or other adverse consequences."
		"pay_page_协议正文_148" "21.5 Matters not covered by this Agreement, event rules, or announcements shall be subject to the latest content published or updated by Yunhai Youchuang through official channels. Yunhai Youchuang reserves the right to formulate, adjust, and publish event rules and related announcements in accordance with laws, regulations, third-party platform rules, and operational requirements. Such rules and announcements take effect according to the time and method specified therein, and the user's continued use of the service constitutes acceptance thereof."
		"pay_page_协议正文_149" "21.6 In the event of any disagreement over the interpretation of this Agreement and related rules or announcements, both parties shall negotiate and resolve it based on the literal meaning of the terms, the purpose of publication, and standard understanding; if negotiation fails, it shall be resolved in accordance with Article 19 of this Agreement."
